## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？

```

```

```

```
오늘은 어떤 걸 해볼까요?
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を保証する通信、UDPは保証しない代わりに速くて軽い通信です。

TCPは届いたか確認して再送し、順番も揃えて届けます。UDPは確認も再送もせず、速さ重視で送ります。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Haz rebase para ordenar tus commits locales sobre una rama base actualizada antes de compartirlos, siempre que nadie más trabaje sobre esa rama. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está publicada o la comparten otras personas, porque reescribir su historial les daría problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 面向连接、可靠、有序，UDP 无连接、不保证送达和顺序，但更快、开销更小。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to catch up with the base and keep history linear. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, since rewriting history other people have pulled causes problems.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接给文件路径就行：`bun test tests/foo.test.ts`。也可以只写文件名的一部分，比如 `bun test foo`，Bun 会跑所有路径里含 `foo` 的测试文件。

如果只想跑某一个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：`bun test tests/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例名"`。
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to a database gets written down before it's applied to the actual data, so a crash mid-write can be replayed or rolled back instead of corrupting things. It trades a little extra write work for durability and fast recovery, since the log is append-only and the main data files can be updated later at leisure.
```

```
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

请在现有分支上继续：
1. 先读 PR 上的 review 意见，找到关于锁粒度的那几条。
2. 把锁从粗粒度改细（只锁真正需要保护的部分），不改其他行为。
3. 跑相关测试，确认没有回归，并报告测试结果。
4. 改动提交在当前分支上，不合并、不推送、不改 PR 的状态。

完成标准：review 里提到的锁粒度问题都改掉，测试通过，报告里说明改了哪些地方。
不在范围内：合并到主分支、推送到远端、关闭或发布 PR。

```

```
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with Nights 2 and 3 filled in, and it will add each new night's result as it comes in.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1484 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">No runs yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Writes/s, p99 latency, compaction stalls</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nightly runs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  function render(runs) {
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var latest = document.getElementById('latest');
    var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    if (!runs || !runs.length) {
      latest.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">No runs yet</span>';
      return;
    }
    var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
    latest.textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
    sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls + ' · ' + last.night;
    runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function (r) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">' + r.night + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' · stalls ' + r.stalls + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + r.writes + '/s</div>';
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  var got = ducter.get('runs');
  Promise.resolve(got).then(render);
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(render);
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}
```

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: the page is on your apps shelf with the four PRs, and ticking one off stays ticked when you come back.
  [an app card under the message: made "My PRs waiting", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2101 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one off as it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var ITEMS = ducter.get("items") || [];
  var done = {};

  function render() {
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    var left = 0;
    ITEMS.forEach(function (it) {
      var row = document.createElement('label');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var box = document.createElement('input');
      box.type = 'checkbox';
      box.className = 'k-check';
      box.checked = !!done[it.id];
      box.addEventListener('change', function () {
        done[it.id] = box.checked;
        ducter.set('done', done);
        render();
      });
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = it.title;
      if (done[it.id]) main.style.textDecoration = 'line-through';
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = it.meta;
      var text = document.createElement('div');
      text.appendChild(main);
      text.appendChild(sub);
      row.appendChild(box);
      row.appendChild(text);
      list.appendChild(row);
      if (!done[it.id]) left++;
    });
    var landed = ITEMS.length - left;
    document.getElementById('left').textContent = left;
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = landed
      ? landed + ' landed, ' + left + ' still waiting'
      : 'Tick each one off as it lands';
    ducter.summary(left + ' waiting');
  }

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function (saved) {
    if (saved && typeof saved === 'object') done = saved;
    render();
  });
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function (saved) {
      if (saved && typeof saved === 'object') done = saved;
      render();
    });
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"Compaction cut","meta":"wey-gu/ducter #412 · waiting on Weizhen’s review since Mon"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"Retry on 502","meta":"nowledge-co/mem #10287 · on CI, failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","meta":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592 · waiting on Alice since Tue"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"Apps bar","meta":"wey-gu/ducter #418 · draft"}]}
```

```
  if(!xs.length){list.append(el("div","k-empty","还没有花销，记一笔就会出现在这里"));}
  xs.forEach(x=>{
    const row=el("div","k-row");
    const main=el("div","k-row-main",(x.cat?x.cat+" · ":"")+(x.trip||""));
    const sub=el("div","k-row-sub",[x.day,x.note].filter(Boolean).join(" · "));
    main.append(sub);
    row.append(main,el("div","k-row-value",fmt(x.amt)));
    list.append(row);
  });
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total):"还没记");
}
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
  cat=b.dataset.cat;
  document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(c=>c.setAttribute("aria-pressed",c===b?"true":"false"));
});
document.getElementById("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=parseFloat(document.getElementById("amt").value);
  if(!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:document.getElementById("trip").value.trim(),day:document.getElementById("day").value.trim(),cat:cat,amt:amt});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  document.getElementById("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

```
在现有的假时钟 TTL 测试基础上，再补一条测试：时钟正好推进到过期时刻（expiry 当刻）时，该值应当视为已过期（或按现有约定的边界语义处理，并在测试名里写清楚）。同时确认刚好早于到期时刻的一毫秒仍然有效。

完成标准：新测试通过，并且连跑 20 次全部通过；报告里给出测试输出。
范围外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR；改动留在现有分支上提交。
工作目录：~/dev/foo
```
